  1149	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1150	        canvas: Canvas,
  1151	        columnX: Float,
  1152	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1153	        stageLeft: Float,
  1154	        stageTop: Float,
  1155	        stageScale: Float,
  1156	        alpha: Float
  1157	    ) {
  1158	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1159	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1160	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1161	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1162	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1163	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1164	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1165	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1166	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1167	        canvas.drawText(
  1168	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1169	            centerX,
  1170	            baselineY,
  1171	            formLabelPaint
  1172	        )
  1173	
  1174	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1175	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1176	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1177	            avatarLeftX,
  1178	            avatarTopY,
  1179	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1180	            scaledAvatarSize
  1181	        )
  1182	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1183	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1184	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1185	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1186	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1187	
  1188	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1189	
  1190	        canvas.save()
  1191	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1192	        if (
  1193	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1194	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1195	            dragDirection != null &&
  1196	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1197	        ) {
  1198	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1199	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1200	                canvas,
  1201	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1202	                avatarBounds,
  1203	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  1204	                stageLeft,
  1205	                stageTop,
  1206	                stageScale,
  1207	                alpha
  1208	            )
  1209	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1210	                canvas,
  1211	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1212	                avatarBounds,
  1213	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  1214	                stageLeft,
  1215	                stageTop,
  1216	                stageScale,
  1217	                alpha
  1218	            )
  1219	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  1220	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1221	                canvas,
  1222	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  1223	                avatarBounds,
  1224	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  1225	                stageLeft,
  1226	                stageTop,
  1227	                stageScale,
  1228	                alpha
  1229	            )
  1230	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1231	                canvas,
  1232	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  1233	                avatarBounds,
  1234	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  1235	                stageLeft,
  1236	                stageTop,
  1237	                stageScale,
  1238	                alpha
  1239	            )
  1240	        } else {
  1241	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1242	                canvas,
  1243	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1244	                avatarBounds,
  1245	                0f,
  1246	                stageLeft,
  1247	                stageTop,
  1248	                stageScale,
  1249	                alpha
  1250	            )
  1251	        }
  1252	        canvas.restore()
  1253	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1254	            canvas,
  1255	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  1256	            avatarBounds,
  1257	            stageLeft,
  1258	            stageTop,
  1259	            stageScale,
  1260	            alpha
  1261	        )
  1262	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1263	            canvas,
  1264	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  1265	            avatarBounds,
  1266	            stageLeft,
  1267	            stageTop,
  1268	            stageScale,
  1269	            alpha
  1270	        )
  1271	    }

  1294	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(
  1295	        canvas: Canvas,
  1296	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1297	        stageLeft: Float,
  1298	        stageTop: Float,
  1299	        stageScale: Float,
  1300	        alpha: Float
  1301	    ) {
  1302	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1303	            canvas,
  1304	            createAvatarBubbleShell,

  1313	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  1314	        canvas: Canvas,
  1315	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1316	        stageLeft: Float,
  1317	        stageTop: Float,
  1318	        stageScale: Float
  1319	    ) {
  1320	        tempPath.reset()
  1321	        tempPath.addCircle(
  1322	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  1323	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  1324	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  1325	            Path.Direction.CW
  1326	        )
  1327	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1328	    }
  1329	
  1330	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  1331	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  1332	    }
  1333	
  1334	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1335	        canvas: Canvas,
  1336	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  1337	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1338	        stageLeft: Float,
  1339	        stageTop: Float,
  1340	        stageScale: Float,
  1341	        alpha: Float
  1342	    ) {
  1343	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1344	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  1345	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1346	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1347	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  1348	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  1349	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1350	
  1351	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  1352	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1353	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1354	        } else {
  1355	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  1356	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  1357	        }
  1358	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1359	    }
  1360	
  1361	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  1362	        canvas: Canvas,
  1363	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  1364	        x: Float,
  1365	        y: Float,
  1366	        stageLeft: Float,
  1367	        stageTop: Float,
  1368	        stageScale: Float,
  1369	        alpha: Float,
  1370	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1371	    ) {
  1372	        tempRect.set(
  1373	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1374	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1375	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  1376	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1377	        )
  1378	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1379	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1380	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1381	
  1382	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1383	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  1384	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1385	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1386	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1387	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1388	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - chevronInset * 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1389	        canvas.drawText(
  1390	            trailingFittingText(mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown), maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  1391	            tempRect.left + textInset,
  1392	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  1393	            formInputPaint
  1394	        )
  1395	
  1396	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  1397	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  1398	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  1399	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1400	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  1401	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1402	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  1403	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1404	    }
  1405	
  1406	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  1407	        canvas: Canvas,
  1408	        stageLeft: Float,
  1409	        stageTop: Float,
  1410	        stageScale: Float,
  1411	        alpha: Float
  1412	    ) {
  1413	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  1414	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  1415	
  1416	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  1417	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  1418	            dropdown,
  1419	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  1420	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1421	        )
  1422	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  1423	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1424	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  1425	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  1426	        val optionAlpha = 255
  1427	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  1428	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1429	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  1430	        } else {
  1431	            0f
  1432	        }
  1433	        val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - scrollbarReservedWidth).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1434	
  1435	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  1436	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  1437	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  1438	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  1439	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  1440	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  1441	            } else {
  1442	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  1443	            }
  1444	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  1445	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  1446	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1447	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  1448	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  1449	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1450	
  1451	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  1452	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  1453	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1454	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1455	            canvas.drawText(
  1456	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  1457	                left + optionTextInset,
  1458	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1459	                formInputPaint
  1460	            )
  1461	        }
  1462	
  1463	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1464	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  1465	                canvas,
  1466	                dropdown,
  1467	                top,
  1468	                right,
  1469	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  1470	                stageScale,
  1471	                optionAlpha
  1472	            )
  1473	        }
  1474	    }
  1475	
  1476	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  1477	        canvas: Canvas,
  1478	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  1479	        top: Float,
  1480	        right: Float,
  1481	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  1482	        stageScale: Float,
  1483	        alpha: Int
  1484	    ) {
  1485	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  1486	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  1487	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  1488	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  1489	
  1490	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  1491	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  1492	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  1493	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  1494	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  1495	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  1496	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  1497	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  1498	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  1499	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1500	
  1501	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  1502	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1503	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1504	
  1505	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  1506	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1507	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1508	    }

  1510	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  1511	        canvas: Canvas,
  1512	        field: MockSignInField,
  1513	        x: Float,
  1514	        y: Float,
  1515	        stageLeft: Float,
  1516	        stageTop: Float,
  1517	        stageScale: Float,
  1518	        alpha: Float,
  1519	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1520	    ) {
  1521	        tempRect.set(
  1522	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1523	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1524	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  1525	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1526	        )
  1527	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1528	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1529	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1530	
  1531	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  1532	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  1533	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1534	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  1535	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  1536	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  1537	            labelProgress
  1538	        ) * stageScale
  1539	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1540	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1541	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  1542	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1543	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1544	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  1545	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  1546	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  1547	            labelProgress
  1548	        )
  1549	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  1550	        canvas.drawText(
  1551	            label,
  1552	            labelX,
  1553	            labelBaselineY,
  1554	            formLabelPaint
  1555	        )
  1556	
  1557	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  1558	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  1559	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  1560	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  1561	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1562	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  1563	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  1564	
  1565	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  1566	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  1567	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1568	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  1569	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  1570	
  1571	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  1572	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  1573	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1574	                canvas.drawLine(
  1575	                    caretX,
  1576	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  1577	                    caretX,
  1578	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  1579	                    formCaretPaint
  1580	                )
  1581	            }
  1582	        }
  1583	    }

  1585	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1586	        canvas: Canvas,
  1587	        x: Float,
  1588	        y: Float,

  1590	        stageTop: Float,
  1591	        stageScale: Float,

  1598	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  1599	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  1600	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  1601	
  1602	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1603	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1604	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  1605	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  1606	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  1607	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  1608	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  1609	
  1610	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  1611	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1612	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  1613	            canvas.drawLine(
  1614	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  1615	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  1616	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  1617	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  1618	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  1619	            )
  1620	            canvas.drawLine(
  1621	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  1622	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  1623	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  1624	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  1625	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  1626	            )
  1627	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  1628	        }
  1629	
  1630	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1631	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1632	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1633	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  1634	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1635	        canvas.drawText(
  1636	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  1637	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  1638	            baselineY,
  1639	            rememberMeTextPaint
  1640	        )
  1641	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  1642	    }

  1644	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  1645	        canvas: Canvas,
  1646	        centerX: Float,
  1647	        topY: Float,
  1648	        stageLeft: Float,
  1649	        stageTop: Float,
  1650	        stageScale: Float,
  1651	        alpha: Float
  1652	    ) {
  1653	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  1654	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1655	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  1656	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1657	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  1658

  1660	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1661	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  1662	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  1663	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  1664	    }

  1666	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  1667	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  1668	    }
  1669	
  1670	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  1671	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  1672	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  1673	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  1674	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  1675	
  1676	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  1677	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  1678	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  1679	        }
  1680	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  1681	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  1682	        }
  1683	    }
  1684	
  1685	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  1686	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  1687	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  1688	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  1689	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  1690	
  1691	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  1692	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  1693	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  1694	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  1695	
  1696	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  1697	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  1698	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  1699	            val dx = x - centerX
  1700	            val dy = y - centerY
  1701	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  1702	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  1703	            } else {
  1704	                pixels[index]
  1705	            }
  1706	        }
  1707	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  1708	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  1709	        }
  1710	    }
  1711	
  1712	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  1713	        pixels: IntArray,
  1714	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  1715	        bitmapHeight: Int
  1716	    ): BooleanArray {
  1717	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  1718	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  1719	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel)
  1720	        }
  1721	
  1722	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  1723	        val outlineRadius = 7
  1724	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  1725	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  1726	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  1727	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  1728	
  1729	                mask[index] = true
  1730	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  1731	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  1732	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  1733	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  1734	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  1735	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  1736	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  1737	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  1738	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  1739	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  1740	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  1741	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  1742	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  1743	                        )
  1744	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  1745	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  1746	                        }
  1747	                    }
  1748	                }
  1749	            }
  1750	        }
  1751	        return mask
  1752	    }
  1753	
  1754	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  1755	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  1756	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  1757	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  1758	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  1759	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  1760	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  1761	        return alpha > 16 && channelMax > 48 && channelMax - channelMin > 22
  1762	    }
  1763	
  1764	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  1765	        return when {
  1766	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  1767	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  1768	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  1769	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  1770	            else -> null
  1771	        }
  1772	    }
  1773	
  1774	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  1775	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  1776	    }
  1777	
  1778	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  1779	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  1780	    }
  1781	
  1782	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  1783	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  1784	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  1785	
  1786	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  1787	
  1788	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  1789	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  1790	            val bounds = target.bounds
  1791	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  1792	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  1793	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  1794	            val dx = x - centerX
  1795	            val dy = y - centerY
  1796	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  1797	        }?.profile
  1798	    }
  1799	
  1800	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  1801	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null || !isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1802	
  1803	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  1804	        val left = stage.left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X * stage.scale
  1805	        val centerY = stage.top +
  1806	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  1807	        val right = left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale
  1808	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  1809	        return x in left..right && y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  1810	    }
  1811	
  1812	    private fun settledSignInPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  1813	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null || !isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1814	
  1815	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  1816	        val centerX = stage.left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stage.scale
  1817	        val centerY = stage.top +
  1818	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  1819	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  1820	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  1821	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  1822	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  1823	    }
  1824	
  1825	    private fun mockSignInFieldHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockSignInField? {
  1826	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return null
  1827	
  1828	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  1829	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  1830	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  1831	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1832	        return activeMockFields().firstOrNull { field ->
  1833	            val fieldX = mockActiveFieldX(field)
  1834	            val fieldY = mockActiveFieldY(field, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1835	            mockSignInFieldContains(x, y, fieldX, fieldY, stage, mockActiveFieldWidth(field))
  1836	        }
  1837	    }

  1839	    private fun mockRememberMeHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  1840	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) return false
  1841	
  1842	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  1843	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  1844	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  1845	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1846	        val fieldX = mockFormX()

  1887	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  1888	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return false
  1889	
  1890	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  1891	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  1892	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  1893	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1894	        val columnX = mockCreateFormX() + (MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS - 1) * (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP)
  1895	        val firstFieldY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY

  1905	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  1906	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  1907	
  1908	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  1909	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  1910	        if (
  1911	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1912	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  1913	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  1914	        ) {
  1915	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  1916	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1917	        }
  1918	
  1919	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  1920	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1921	        }
  1922	        return true
  1923	    }

  1925	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  1926	        if (
  1927	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  1928	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  1929	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  1930	        ) {
  1931	            return null
  1932	        }
  1933	
  1934	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  1935	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  1936	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  1937	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  1938	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1939	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  1940	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1941	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  1942	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  1943	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  1944	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  1945	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  1946	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  1947	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  1948	        }
  1949	
  1950	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  1951	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  1952	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  1953	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  1954	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  1955	        }
  1956	        return null
  1957	    }
  1958	
  1959	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  1960	        if (
  1961	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  1962	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  1963	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  1964	        ) {
  1965	            return null
  1966	        }
  1967	
  1968	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  1969	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  1970	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  1971	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  1972	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  1973	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  1974	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1975	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  1976	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  1977	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  1978	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  1979	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  1980	    }

  1982	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  1983	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  1984	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  1985	
  1986	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  1987	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  1988	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  1989	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  1990	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  1991	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1992	        }
  1993	
  1994	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  1995	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  1996	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  1997	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1998	        }
  1999	        return true
  2000	    }

  2002	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2003	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2004	
  2005	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2006	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2007	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)

  2009	        val lastField = activeMockFields().lastOrNull() ?: return false
  2010	        val lastFieldY = mockActiveFieldY(lastField, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2011	        val chevronTopY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.SignIn) {
  2012	            lastFieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE +
  2013	                MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2014	        } else {
  2015	            MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY + mockCreateFormHeight() + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2016	        }
  2017	        return mockBackChevronContains(x, y, mockActiveFormCenterX(), chevronTopY, stage)
  2018	    }

  2020	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2021	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2022	
  2023	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2024	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2025	
  2026	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2027	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2028	    }
  2029	
  2030	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  2031	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  2032	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2033	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2034	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2035	    }

  2037	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
  2038	        pointerX: Float,
  2039	        pointerY: Float,

  2041	        fieldY: Float,

  2043	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2044	    ): Boolean {
  2045	        val left = stage.left + fieldX * stage.scale
  2046	        val top = stage.top + fieldY * stage.scale
  2047	        val right = stage.left + (fieldX + fieldWidth) * stage.scale
  2048	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  2049	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2050	    }

  2052	    private fun mockRememberMeContains(
  2053	        pointerX: Float,
  2054	        pointerY: Float,
  2055	        x: Float,
  2056	        y: Float,
  2057	        stage: StageMetrics
  2058	    ): Boolean {
  2059	        val left = stage.left + x * stage.scale
  2060	        val top = stage.top + (y - MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_TOP_PADDING) * stage.scale
  2061	        val right = stage.left + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stage.scale
  2062	        val bottom = stage.top + (y - MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_TOP_PADDING + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  2063	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2064	    }

  2066	    private fun mockBackChevronContains(
  2067	        pointerX: Float,
  2068	        pointerY: Float,
  2069	        centerX: Float,
  2070	        topY: Float,
  2071	        stage: StageMetrics
  2072	    ): Boolean {

  2074	        val hitHalfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2075	        val left = center - hitHalfWidth
  2076	        val right = center + hitHalfWidth
  2077	        val top = stage.top + (topY - (MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT - MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT) / 2f) * stage.scale
  2078	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale
  2079	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  2080	    }

  2082	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX: Float, firstFieldY: Float): Bounds {
  2083	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2084	        return Bounds(
  2085	            columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f,
  2086	            firstFieldY +
  2087	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE +
  2088	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE,
  2089	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2090	            scaledAvatarSize
  2091	        )
  2092	    }

  2094	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  2095	        columnX: Float,
  2096	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2097	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2098	    ): Bounds {
  2099	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  2100	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  2101	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  2102	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  2103	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  2104	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  2105	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  2106	        return Bounds(
  2107	            centerX - side / 2f,
  2108	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  2109	            side,
  2110	            side
  2111	        )
  2112	    }

  2133	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  2134	        pointerX: Float,
  2135	        pointerY: Float,
  2136	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2137	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2138	        stage: StageMetrics
  2139	    ): Boolean {
  2140	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2141	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  2142	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2143	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2144	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2145	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2146	    }

  2148	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  2149	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  2150	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  2151	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  2152	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  2153	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  2154	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  2155	            activeComposingText = ""
  2156	            focusedMockField = field
  2157	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  2158	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2159	        }
  2160	        requestFocus()
  2161	        post {
  2162	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2163	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  2164	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  2165	        }
  2166	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2167	    }

  2341	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {

  2344	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT

  2351	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  2352	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2353	    }

  2355	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  2356	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  2357	    }
  2358	
  2359	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  2360	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  2361	    }

  2363	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  2364	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  2365	    }

  2368	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2369	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2370	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  2371	    }
  2372	
  2373	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  2374	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2375	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  2376	            return
  2377	        }
  2378	
  2379	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  2380	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  2381	    }

  2419	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  2420	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2421	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  2422	    ) {
  2423	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  2424	
  2425	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  2426	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  2427

  2430	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = nextIndex

  2433	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  2434	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  2435	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2436	    }

  2503	    private fun updateMockFormLabelAnimation() {
  2504	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  2505	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormLabelAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  2506	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = now
  2507	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()
  2508	        mockFieldLabelFloatProgress.keys.forEach { field ->
  2509	            mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] = moveToward(
  2510	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,
  2511	                mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field),
  2512	                step
  2513	            )
  2514	        }
  2515	    }

  2533	    private fun updateMockInputDimAnimation() {

  2535	        if (target > 0f) {
  2536	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = expandedMockDropdown
  2537	            mockInputDimActiveField = if (expandedMockDropdown == null) focusedMockField else null
  2538	        }
  2539	
  2540	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  2541	        val elapsed = (now - lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  2542	        lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis = now
  2543	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_INPUT_DIM_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  2544	        mockInputDimProgress = moveToward(mockInputDimProgress, target, step)
  2545	
  2546	        if (mockInputDimProgress == 0f && target == 0f) {
  2547	            mockInputDimActiveField = null
  2548	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  2549	        }
  2550	    }

  2586	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  2587	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  2588	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  2589	        return if (
  2590	            focusedMockField != null ||
  2591	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  2592	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  2593	        ) {
  2594	            1f
  2595	        } else {
  2596	            0f
  2597	        }
  2598	    }

  2600	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  2601	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  2602	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  2603	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  2604	    }
  2605	
  2606	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  2607	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  2608	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  2609	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  2610	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  2611	        }
  2612	
  2613	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  2614	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  2615	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  2616	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  2617	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  2618	    }
  2619	
  2620	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {
  2621	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  2622	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f
  2623	        val awaitingField = mockInputDimAwaitingField ?: return 0f
  2624	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  2625	    }

  2627	    private fun mockFormFocusTarget(): Float {

  2630	            return if (focusedMockField != null || expandedMockDropdown != null) 1f else 0f
  2631	        }
  2632	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return 0f
  2633	        return if (
  2634	            focusedMockField != null ||
  2635	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }
  2636	        ) {
  2637	            1f
  2638	        } else {
  2639	            0f
  2640	        }
  2641	    }

  2651	            -(LOGO_FINAL_TOP + LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT + 64f) * liftMotion

  2701	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {

  2703	        when (field) {

  2705	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  2706	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  2707	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  2708	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  2709	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  2710	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  2711	        }
  2712	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  2713	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  2714	        }
  2715	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  2716	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2717	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()

  2721	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  2722	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  2723	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  2724	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  2725	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  2726	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  2727	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  2728	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  2729	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  2730	        activeComposingText = ""
  2731	        return true
  2732	    }
  2733	
  2734	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  2735	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  2736	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  2737	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  2738	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  2739	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  2740	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  2741	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  2742	        activeComposingText = composingText
  2743	        return true
  2744	    }
  2745	
  2746	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  2747	        activeComposingText = ""
  2748	        return true
  2749	    }
  2750	
  2751	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  2752	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  2753	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  2754	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  2755	            activeComposingText = ""
  2756	            return true
  2757	        }
  2758	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  2759	        activeComposingText = ""
  2760	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  2761	        return true
  2762	    }
  2763	
  2764	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  2765	        activeComposingText = ""
  2766	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  2767	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  2768	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  2769	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  2770	        } else {
  2771	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2772	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  2773	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  2774	        }
  2775	        return true
  2776	    }
  2777	
  2778	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  2779	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  2780	        return when (keyCode) {
  2781	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  2782	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  2783	            else -> {
  2784	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  2785	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  2786	            }
  2787	        }
  2788	    }
  2789	
  2790	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  2791	        return buildString {
  2792	            input.forEach { character ->
  2793	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  2794	                    append(character)
  2795	                }
  2796	            }
  2797	        }
  2798	    }
  2799	
  2800	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  2801	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  2802	
  2803	        var startIndex = 0
  2804	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  2805	            startIndex += 1
  2806	        }
  2807	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  2808	    }
  2809	
  2810	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  2811	        return when {
  2812	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  2813	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  2814	            else -> current
  2815	        }
  2816	    }
  2817	
  2818	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  2819	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  2820	
  2821	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  2822	        return StageMetrics(
  2823	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  2824	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f + mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  2825	            scale = stageScale
  2826	        )
  2827	    }

  2868	        pressedMockField = null
  2869	        pressedRememberMe = false
  2870	        pressedBackChevron = false
  2871	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2872	        pressedMockDropdown = null

  2877	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null

  2889	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
  2890	        }

  2893	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2894	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()

  2896	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager

  2898	        clearFocus()
  2899	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  2900	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress

  2904	
  2905	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  2906	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  2907	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  2908	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2909	        return lerpFloat(
  2910	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  2911	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  2912	            progress
  2913	        )
  2914	    }
  2915	
  2916	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  2917	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  2918	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  2919	    }
  2920	
  2921	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  2922	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  2923	    }
  2924	
  2925	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  2926	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  2927	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  2928	            .roundToInt()
  2929	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  2930	            .toLong()
  2931	    }
  2932	
  2933	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  2934	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  2935	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  2936	
  2937	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  2938	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  2939	            activeMockFlow = null
  2940	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  2941	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  2942	            pressedMockField = null
  2943	            pressedRememberMe = false
  2944	            pressedBackChevron = false
  2945	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2946	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  2947	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2948	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  2949	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  2950	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  2951	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  2952	            return
  2953	        }
  2954	
  2955	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  2956	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  2957	    }
  2958	
  2959	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  2960	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  2961	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  2962	        }
  2963

  2966	        }

  2970	        }
  2971

  2973	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  2974	        }
  2975	
  2976	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  2977	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  2978	        }
  2979	
  2980	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  2981	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  2982	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  2983	        }
  2984	
  2985	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  2986	            return ""
  2987	        }
  2988	
  2989	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  2990	            return ""
  2991	        }
  2992	
  2993	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  2994	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  2995	        }
  2996	
  2997	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  2998	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  2999	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  3000	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3001	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3002	                else -> {
  3003	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3004	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  3005	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  3006	                    } else {
  3007	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  3008	                    }
  3009	                }
  3010	            }
  3011	        }
  3012	    }
  3013	}

  3104	
  3105	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  3106	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  3107	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3108	}
  3109	
  3110	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3111	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  3112	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3113	}
  3114	
  3115	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3116	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  3117	}
  3118	
  3119	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  3120	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  3121	}
  3122	
  3123	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  3124	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3125	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  3126	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  3127	    return base + bounce
  3128	}
  3129	
  3130	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  3131	
  3132	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  3133	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  3134	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  3135	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  3136	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  3137	)
  3138	
  3139	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  3140	    x = bounds.x,
  3141	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  3142	    w = bounds.w,
  3143	    h = bounds.h
  3144	)
  3145	
  3146	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  3147	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3148	    return Color.argb(
  3149	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  3150	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  3151	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  3152	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  3153	    )
  3154	}
  3155	
  3156	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  3157	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  3158	}
  3159	
  3160	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3161	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  3162	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  3163	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  3164	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  3165	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  3166	    return output
  3167	}
  3168	
  3169	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3170	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  3171	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  3172	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  3173	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  3174	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  3175	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  3176	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  3177	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  3178	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  3179	    return output
  3180	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
